{"type":"assistant.message","data":{"messageId":"e80d2a0b-89cc-49fc-b7ba-fee557335dda","content":"The customer is **not supposed to authenticate**.\n\nThe authentication belongs to the **owner/admin** using the application:\n\n```text\nOwner registers/logs in\n        ↓\nBackend returns JWT token\n        ↓\nFrontend stores token in localStorage\n        ↓\nFrontend sends token with customer requests\n        ↓\nBackend verifies token before creating customers\n```\n\nWhen you add a customer, the request is:\n\n```http\nPOST /customers\nAuthorization: Bearer YOUR_OWNER_JWT\n```\n\nThe backend protects the endpoint with:\n\n```python\n_: User = Depends(current_user)\n```\n\nThe backend checks:\n\n1. Is an `Authorization` header present?\n2. Is it a valid JWT?\n3. Is the token expired?\n4. Does the user still exist in the database?\n\nIf any check fails, FastAPI returns:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"detail\": \"Not authenticated\"\n}\n```\n\nor:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"detail\": \"Invalid or expired token\"\n}\n```\n\nThe customer only has a database record:\n\n- name\n- phone\n- address\n- plan price\n- subscription start date\n- pause history\n\nThey do not currently have a separate login.\n\n**What to do now**\n\n1. Open the frontend:\n   `http://localhost:5174/`\n\n2. Log out if you are already inside the app.\n\n3. Register or log in again.\n\n4. After login, add the customer.\n\n5. Do not open the frontend using a different port or old tab. Use the current URL:\n   `http://localhost:5174/`\n\nYou can verify authentication manually in the API docs:\n\n1. Open `http://localhost:5174/api/docs`.\n2. Call `POST /auth/register` or `POST /auth/login`.\n3. Copy the returned `access_token`.\n4. Click **Authorize**.\n5. Enter:\n\n```text\nBearer YOUR_ACCESS_TOKEN\n```\n\n6. Then call `POST /customers`.\n\nThe frontend already adds the token here:\n\n```javascript\nconst token = localStorage.getItem('tiffin_token')\nAuthorization: `Bearer ${token}`\n```\n\nSo “Not authenticated” means the owner login token is missing or invalid, not that the customer needs to log in.","toolRequests":[],"reasoningText":""},"id":"8d18ce08-f6b3-4556-b4bb-206e68d9f7e7","timestamp":"2026-09-17T10:31:14.351Z","parentId":"3d339215-09e5-405f-89ce-e5db61a355b1"}
{"type":"assistant.turn_end","data":{"turnId":"1"},"id":"20165716-09d2-49da-876b-772eb4833bd6","timestamp":"2026-09-17T10:31:14.351Z","parentId":"8d18ce08-f6b3-4556-b4bb-206e68d9f7e7"}
{"type":"user.message","data":{"content":"🚨 CRITICAL UPDATE — MY ASSIGNED PROBLEM HAS 3 TWISTS.\n\nThe following twists are PART OF MY ACTUAL ASSIGNED PROBLEM and MUST be implemented.\n\nDo NOT treat them as optional.\nDo NOT postpone them until the very end.\nDesign the database, APIs and billing logic so these twists are supported correctly from the beginning.\n\nThe evaluation will specifically test these behaviors.\n\n# ==================================================\nBASE PROBLEM\nThis is a home-style tiffin/lunch delivery service.\n\nCustomers subscribe to a monthly plan and receive lunch every weekday.\n\nCustomers can pause their subscription for some days and should not be charged for days when they were not served.\n\nAt month-end, the owner needs each customer's prorated bill based on the actual days served.\n\nCustomers are searched by phone.\n\nThe owner needs to see ACTIVE vs PAUSED customers.\n\nCore flow:\n\nSUBSCRIBE → PAUSE/RESUME → DELIVER → BILL\n\nNow there are 3 twists.\n\n# ==================================================\nTWIST 1 — LEVEL 1 — T1 — INTEGRATION\nRequirement:\n\n\"Each morning, notify the customers due a delivery today (active, a weekday, not paused) via the Notification Service.\"\n\nEvaluation:\n\nThe evaluator will use:\n\nPOST /clock\n\nand then inspect:\n\n/outbox\n\nThis means this is NOT just a UI feature.\n\nThere must be an actual integration-style workflow.\n\nIMPORTANT:\n\nWhen POST /clock advances/simulates the current date/time, the system must identify all customers who are due a delivery on that date.\n\nA customer is DUE TODAY only if:\n\n1. Their subscription is active on that date\n2. The date is Monday-Friday\n3. They are NOT paused on that date\nFor every eligible customer, create/send a notification through the Notification Service.\n\nThe evaluator will verify the resulting notification through /outbox.\n\n# ==================================================\nT1 — REQUIRED DESIGN\nImplement a Notification Service abstraction.\n\nIf the challenge environment provides a real Notification Service endpoint/API, inspect the available environment/configuration and integrate with it.\n\nIf the challenge provides a specific Notification Service contract, FOLLOW THAT CONTRACT EXACTLY.\n\nDo NOT invent a conflicting API.\n\nThe application should have a clear integration layer such as:\n\nnotification_service.py\n\nor equivalent.\n\nThe business logic should call something like:\n\nnotify_customer_due_for_delivery(customer, delivery_date)\n\nThe notification should contain useful information such as:\n\n- customer\n- phone\n- delivery date\n- delivery/tiffin due message\nDo NOT send notifications on weekends.\n\nDo NOT notify paused customers.\n\nDo NOT notify customers whose subscription has not started.\n\nDo NOT notify customers whose subscription is no longer active.\n\n# ==================================================\nPOST /clock\nImplement/use:\n\nPOST /clock\n\naccording to the challenge's expected contract.\n\nIMPORTANT:\n\nDo not assume POST /clock simply changes a variable.\n\nIt must trigger the \"morning delivery notification\" workflow for the resulting/current simulated date if that is how the challenge contract defines it.\n\nExample:\n\nPOST /clock\n\n→ determine today's date\n\n→ determine weekday\n\n→ find active subscriptions\n\n→ remove paused customers\n\n→ identify customers due delivery\n\n→ notify them through Notification Service\n\n→ notification becomes visible in /outbox\n\nThe operation should be safe to test repeatedly.\n\nAvoid accidental duplicate notifications for the same customer/date if the expected challenge semantics require idempotency.\n\n# ==================================================\nT1 EXAMPLE\nSuppose:\n\nMonday = September 14\n\nCustomer A:\nActive\nNot paused\n\nCustomer B:\nPaused September 14-16\n\nCustomer C:\nSubscription starts September 20\n\nCustomer D:\nActive\n\nAfter:\n\nPOST /clock\n\nExpected:\n\nNotification → Customer A\nNotification → Customer D\n\nNo notification → Customer B\nNo notification → Customer C\n\nIf the day is Saturday/Sunday:\n\nNo delivery notifications should be generated.\n\n# ==================================================\nTWIST 2 — LEVEL 2 — T6 — SUBSCRIPTION LIFECYCLE\nRequirement:\n\n\"Transfer a subscription to a new customer mid-cycle; the plan and cycle carry over, billing splits by who was served.\"\n\nThis is a MAJOR billing requirement.\n\nDo NOT simply change the existing customer's name/phone.\n\nThe system must preserve the history of who was actually served.\n\n# ==================================================\nT6 CONCEPT\nExample:\n\nCustomer A has:\n\nMonthly plan = ₹2200\n\nSubscription cycle:\nSeptember 1 → September 30\n\nCustomer A receives lunch from:\n\nSeptember 1 → September 15\n\nThen the subscription is transferred to Customer B on:\n\nSeptember 16\n\nCustomer B receives lunch from:\n\nSeptember 16 → September 30\n\nThe SAME subscription plan/cycle continues.\n\nThe plan does NOT restart for Customer B.\n\nThe billing for September must be split according to who was served.\n\nCustomer A gets charged for the eligible delivery days they received.\n\nCustomer B gets charged for the eligible delivery days they received.\n\nThe combined billing should represent the original subscription's prorated monthly charge.\n\n# ==================================================\nT6 — IMPORTANT DATABASE DESIGN\nDo NOT model subscription ownership in a way that destroys historical ownership.\n\nInstead, introduce a concept such as:\n\nSubscription\n\n- id\n- plan_price\n- cycle_start\n- cycle_end\n- status\n- created_at\nSubscriptionAssignment / SubscriptionCustomerPeriod\n\n- id\n- subscription_id\n- customer_id\n- start_date\n- end_date\nThe exact naming is up to you.\n\nThe important concept is:\n\nONE SUBSCRIPTION/CYCLE\n\ncan have multiple customer ownership periods.\n\nExample:\n\nSubscription #1\n₹2200\nSep 1 → Sep 30\n\nAssignment 1:\nCustomer A\nSep 1 → Sep 15\n\nAssignment 2:\nCustomer B\nSep 16 → Sep 30\n\nThis preserves the actual service history.\n\n# ==================================================\nT6 — TRANSFER API\nImplement a clear REST API.\n\nFor example:\n\nPOST /subscriptions/{subscription_id}/transfer\n\nRequest:\n\n{\n\"new_customer_id\": 123,\n\"transfer_date\": \"2026-09-16\"\n}\n\nYou may use a different endpoint if appropriate, but the operation MUST clearly represent subscription transfer.\n\nValidate:\n\n- subscription exists\n- new customer exists\n- transfer date belongs to the subscription cycle\n- transfer does not create impossible/overlapping ownership periods\n- transfer cannot arbitrarily rewrite past history\n- the existing customer owns the subscription before transfer\nAt transfer:\n\nOLD CUSTOMER:\nend_date = transfer_date - 1 day\n\nNEW CUSTOMER:\nstart_date = transfer_date\n\nThe plan price remains unchanged.\n\nThe cycle remains unchanged.\n\n# ==================================================\nT6 — BILLING\nBilling must now understand ownership periods.\n\nFor every weekday in the billing month:\n\n1. Determine whether the subscription was active.\n2. Determine which customer owned the subscription on that date.\n3. Determine whether that customer was paused on that date.\n4. If served, count that day for that customer.\nExample:\n\nSeptember has 22 weekdays.\n\nPlan = ₹2200.\n\nCustomer A served 10 eligible weekdays.\n\nCustomer B served 9 eligible weekdays.\n\nOther days may be paused/non-delivery.\n\nThe bill must split according to actual service.\n\nReturn billing details that make this transparent.\n\nFor example:\n\n{\n\"month\": \"2026-09\",\n\"subscription_id\": 1,\n\"plan_price\": 2200,\n\"total_weekdays\": 22,\n\"customers\": [\n{\n\"customer_id\": 1,\n\"customer_name\": \"Customer A\",\n\"delivered_weekdays\": 10,\n\"amount\": 1000\n},\n{\n\"customer_id\": 2,\n\"customer_name\": \"Customer B\",\n\"delivered_weekdays\": 9,\n\"amount\": 900\n}\n]\n}\n\nUse the exact mathematically correct calculation based on the original plan price and delivery days.\n\nAvoid rounding errors.\n\nDocument the rounding policy.\n\n# ==================================================\nT6 — CRITICAL RULE\nTRANSFER IS NOT A NEW SUBSCRIPTION.\n\nDo NOT:\n\n- create a new billing cycle\n- reset the monthly plan\n- charge the new customer for a full month\n- delete the old customer's history\n- simply overwrite customer_id\n- lose pause/delivery history\nThe original subscription cycle must continue.\n\n# ==================================================\nTWIST 3 — LEVEL 3 — T4 — MESSY DATA IMPORT\nRequirement:\n\n\"Import a messy customer list (dup phones, mixed date formats, blanks) into clean subscriptions with an { imported, deduped, rejected } report.\"\n\nThis must be implemented as an import workflow.\n\n# ==================================================\nT4 — IMPORT INPUT\nSupport importing a customer list, preferably CSV.\n\nThe data may contain:\n\n- duplicate phone numbers\n- different date formats\n- missing names\n- missing phone numbers\n- missing plan prices\n- invalid dates\n- malformed rows\n- inconsistent whitespace\n- inconsistent phone formatting\nExample messy data:\n\nname,phone,plan_price,start_date\nRahul,9876543210,2200,2026-09-01\nRahul Sharma,987-654-3210,2200,01/09/2026\nPriya,,2200,2026-09-02\nAmit,9999999999,,09-05-2026\nNeha,8888888888,2500,2026-09-10\n\n# ==================================================\nT4 — NORMALIZATION\nNormalize data before insertion.\n\nPhone normalization should handle reasonable formatting differences.\n\nExample:\n\n987-654-3210\n\nand\n\n9876543210\n\nshould be recognized as the same phone number if they represent the same number.\n\nTrim whitespace.\n\nNormalize date formats.\n\nSupport reasonable common formats such as:\n\nYYYY-MM-DD\nDD/MM/YYYY\nDD-MM-YYYY\n\nDo NOT silently guess ambiguous dates if the interpretation cannot be safely determined.\n\nInvalid/ambiguous records should be rejected and reported.\n\nValidate:\n\n- phone\n- name\n- plan price\n- subscription start date\n\n# ==================================================\nT4 — DEDUPLICATION\nDuplicate phones must NOT create duplicate customers/subscriptions unintentionally.\n\nThe import result MUST distinguish:\n\nimported\ndeduped\nrejected\n\nExample:\n\n{\n\"imported\": 7,\n\"deduped\": 2,\n\"rejected\": 3\n}\n\nAlso return useful details for rejected/deduplicated rows if practical.\n\nExample:\n\n{\n\"imported\": 7,\n\"deduped\": 2,\n\"rejected\": 3,\n\"details\": {\n\"deduped\": [\n{\n\"row\": 4,\n\"phone\": \"9876543210\",\n\"reason\": \"Duplicate phone\"\n}\n],\n\"rejected\": [\n{\n\"row\": 6,\n\"reason\": \"Missing phone\"\n}\n]\n}\n}\n\n# ==================================================\nT4 — IMPORT API\nImplement something like:\n\nPOST /customers/import\n\nusing multipart/form-data CSV upload.\n\nOR use the exact import contract expected by the challenge if one is provided.\n\nThe API must:\n\n1. DAYS\n↓\nPAUSES\n↓\nOWNERSHIP PERIOD\n↓\nPRORATED BILL\n\nAnd:\n\nMESSY CSV\n↓\nNORMALIZATION\n↓\nDEDUPLICATION\n↓\nVALIDATION\n↓\nCLEAN CUSTOMER + SUBSCRIPTION\n↓\nNORMAL APPLICATION FLOW\n\n# Weekday + active + not paused → notification created\n2. Weekend → no notification\n3. Paused weekday → no notification\n4. Subscription not started → no notification\n5. Notification eligibility\nDo NOT let frontend state become the source of truth.\n\nThe backend/database must determine:\n\n- active vs paused\n- who owns a subscription\n- who should receive {"type":"assistant.message","data":{"messageId":"2f2b91fe-b4c2-4819-8790-ad1d5ab6e24d","content":"","toolRequests":[{"toolCallId":"call_Bpss7NwG8WUvggWRLZaKkwML","name":"create_file","arguments":"{\"filePath\":\"/workspaces/Tiffinsub/backend/app/billing.py\",\"content\":\"from calendar import monthrange\\nfrom datetime import date, timedelta\\nfrom decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_UP\\n\\n\\ndef weekdays_in_range(start: date, end: date) -> list[date]:\\n    if end < start:\\n        return []\\n    days = []\\n    cursor = start\\n    while cursor <= end:\\n        if cursor.weekday() < 5:\\n            days.append(cursor)\\n        cursor += timedelta(days=1)\\n    return days\\n\\n\\ndef _amount(plan_price: Decimal, delivered: int, total_weekdays: int) -> Decimal:\\n    if not total_weekdays:\\n        return Decimal(\\\"0.00\\\")\\n    return (Decimal(plan_price) * Decimal(delivered) / Decimal(total_weekdays)).quantize(Decimal(\\\"0.01\\\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)\\n\\n\\ndef calculate_subscription_bill(subscription, year: int, month: int) -> dict:\\n    month_start = date(year, month, 1)\\n    month_end = date(year, month, monthrange(year, month)[1])\\n    total_weekdays = len(weekdays_in_range(month_start, month_end))\\n    customers = []\\n    delivered_total = 0\\n    for assignment in sorted(subscription.assignments, key=lambda item: item.start_date):\\n        service_start = max(assignment.start_date, subscription.cycle_start, month_start)\\n        service_end = min(assignment.end_date, subscription.cycle_end, month_end)\\n        service_days = set(weekdays_in_range(service_start, service_end))\\n        paused_days = set()\\n        for pause in assignment.customer.pauses:\\n            paused_days.update(weekdays_in_range(max(pause.start_date, month_start), min(pause.end_date, month_end)))\\n        delivered = len(service_days - paused_days)\\n        delivered_total += delivered\\n        customers.append({\\\"customer_id\\\": assignment.customer_id, \\\"customer_name\\\": assignment.customer.name, \\\"delivered_weekdays\\\": delivered, \\\"amount\\\": _amount(subscription.plan_price, delivered, total_weekdays)})\\n    return {\\\"month\\\": f\\\"{year:04d}-{month:02d}\\\", \\\"subscription_id\\\": subscription.id, \\\"plan_price\\\": Decimal(subscription.plan_price).quantize(Decimal(\\\"0.01\\\")), \\\"total_weekdays\\\": total_weekdays, \\\"paused_weekdays\\\": total_weekdays - delivered_total, \\\"delivered_weekdays\\\": delivered_total, \\\"amount\\\": _amount(subscription.plan_price, delivered_total, total_weekdays), \\\"customers\\\": customers}\\n\\n\\ndef calculate_monthly_bill(customer, year: int, month: int) -> dict:\\n    subscriptions = []\\n    for assignment in getattr(customer, \\\"assignments\\\", []):\\n        if assignment.subscription not in subscriptions:\\n            subscriptions.append(assignment.subscription)\\n    if subscriptions:\\n        result = calculate_subscription_bill(subscriptions[0], year, month)\\n        own = next((item for item in result[\\\"customers\\\"] if item[\\\"customer_id\\\"] == customer.id), None)\\n        return {**result, \\\"delivered_weekdays\\\": own[\\\"delivered_weekdays\\\"] if own else 0, \\\"amount\\\": own[\\\"amount\\\"] if own else Decimal(\\\"0.00\\\"), \\\"customers\\\": result[\\\"customers\\\"]}\\n    month_start = date(year, month, 1)\\n    month_end = date(year, month, monthrange(year, month)[1])\\n    total_weekdays = len(weekdays_in_range(month_start, month_end))\\n    service_days = set(weekdays_in_range(max(customer.subscription_start_date, month_start), month_end))\\n    paused_days = set()\\n    for pause in customer.pauses:\\n        paused_days.update(weekdays_in_range(max(pause.start_date, month_start), min(pause.end_date, month_end)))\\n    paused_days &= service_days\\n    delivered = len(service_days - paused_days)\\n    return {\\\"month\\\": f\\\"{year:04d}-{month:02d}\\\", \\\"subscription_id\\\": None, \\\"plan_price\\\": Decimal(customer.plan_price).quantize(Decimal(\\\"0.01\\\")), \\\"total_weekdays\\\": total_weekdays, \\\"paused_weekdays\\\": len(paused_days), \\\"delivered_weekdays\\\": delivered, \\\"amount\\\": _amount(customer.plan_price, delivered, total_weekdays), \\\"customers\\\": [{\\\"customer_id\\\": customer.id, \\\"customer_name\\\": customer.name, \\\"delivered_weekdays\\\": delivered, \\\"amount\\\": _amount(customer.plan_price, delivered, total_weekdays)}]}\\n\"}","type":"function"}],"reasoningText":""},"id":"9dee67e4-7bd8-41b1-b83e-8ee001bcf493","timestamp":"2026-09-17T10:37:25.721Z","parentId":"4d022dd0-fc60-41b4-9bcd-222361032cf2"}

paused_days)\n        delivered_total += delivered\n        customers.append({\"customer_id\": assignment.customer_id, \"customer_name\": assignment.customer.name, \"delivered_weekdays\": delivered, \"amount\": _amount(subscription.plan_price, delivered, total_weekdays)})\n    return {\"month\": f\"{year:04d}-{month:02d}\", \"subscription_id\": subscription.id, \"plan_price\": Decimal(subscription.plan_price).quantize(Decimal(\"0.01\")), \"total_weekdays\": total_weekdays, \"paused_weekdays\": total_weekdays - delivered_total, \"delivered_weekdays\": delivered_total, \"amount\": _amount(subscription.plan_price, delivered_total, total_weekdays), \"customers\": customers}\n\n\ndef calculate_monthly_bill(customer, year: int, month: int) -> dict:\n    subscriptions = []\n    for assignment in getattr(customer, \"assignments\", []):\n        if assignment.subscription not in subscriptions:\n            subscriptions.append(assignment.subscription)\n    if subscriptions:\n        result = calculate_subscription_bill(subscriptions[0], year, month)\n        own = next((item for item in result[\"customers\"] if item[\"customer_id\"] == customer.id), None)\n        return {**result, \"delivered_weekdays\": own[\"delivered_weekdays\"] if own else 0, \"amount\": own[\"amount\"] if own else Decimal(\"0.00\"), \"customers\": result[\"customers\"]}\n    month_start = date(year, month, 1)\n    month_end = date(year, month, monthrange(year, month)[1])\n    total_weekdays = len(weekdays_in_range(month_start, month_end))\n    service_days = set(weekdays_in_range(max(customer.subscription_start_date, month_start), month_end))\n    paused_days = set()\n    for pause in customer.pauses:\n        paused_days.update(weekdays_in_range(max(pause.start_date, month_start), min(pause.end_date, month_end)))\n    paused_days &= service_days\n    delivered = len(service_days - paused_days)\n    return {\"month\": f\"{year:04d}-{month:02d}\", \"subscription_id\": None, \"plan_price\": Decimal(customer.plan_price).quantize(Decimal(\"0.01\")), \"total_weekdays\": total_weekdays, \"paused_weekdays\": len(paused_days), \"delivered_weekdays\": delivered, \"amount\": _amount(customer.plan_price, delivered, total_weekdays), \"customers\": [{\"customer_id\": customer.id, \"customer_name\": customer.name, \"delivered_weekdays\": delivered, \"amount\": _amount(customer.plan_price, delivered, total_weekdays)}]}\n"}},"id":"ff45fd90-2c28-4112-8125-d450473200c0","timestamp":"2026-09-17T10:37:25.943Z","parentId":"58bfca5f-a445-413b-9159-9d9b12e92aa3"}

delivered_total, \"delivered_weekdays\": delivered_total, \"amount\": _amount(subscription.plan_price, delivered_total, total_weekdays), \"customers\": customers}\n\n\ndef calculate_monthly_bill(customer, year: int, month: int) -> dict:\n    subscriptions = []\n    for assignment in getattr(customer, \"assignments\", []):\n        if assignment.subscription not in subscriptions:\n            subscriptions.append(assignment.subscription)\n    if subscriptions:\n        result = calculate_subscription_bill(subscriptions[0], year, month)\n        own = next((item for item in result[\"customers\"] if item[\"customer_id\"] == customer.id), None)\n        return {**result, \"delivered_weekdays\": own[\"delivered_weekdays\"] if own else 0, \"amount\": own[\"amount\"] if own else Decimal(\"0.00\"), \"customers\": result[\"customers\"]}\n    month_start = date(year, month, 1)\n    month_end = date(year, month, monthrange(year, month)[1])\n    total_weekdays = len(weekdays_in_range(month_start, month_end))\n    service_days = set(weekdays_in_range(max(customer.subscription_start_date, month_start), month_end))\n    paused_days = set()\n    for pause in customer.pauses:\n        paused_days.update(weekdays_in_range(max(pause.start_date, month_start), min(pause.end_date, month_end)))\n    paused_days &= service_days\n    delivered = len(service_days - paused_days)\n    return {\"month\": f\"{year:04d}-{month:02d}\", \"subscription_id\": None, \"plan_price\": Decimal(customer.plan_price).quantize(Decimal(\"0.01\")), \"total_weekdays\": total_weekdays, \"paused_weekdays\": len(paused_days), \"delivered_weekdays\": delivered, \"amount\": _amount(customer.plan_price, delivered, total_weekdays), \"customers\": [{\"customer_id\": customer.id, \"customer_name\": customer.name, \"delivered_weekdays\": delivered, \"amount\": _amount(customer.plan_price, delivered, total_weekdays)}]}\n"}},"id":"d198f629-bd5d-44ff-917f-2cb5a52c0113","timestamp":"2026-09-17T10:37:39.108Z","parentId":"aac07f12-ffc9-43a4-831e-72ff32f34211"}

HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\\\"CSV must be UTF-8 text\\\")\\n+    imported = 0; deduped = 0; rejected = 0; seen = set(); deduped_details = []; rejected_details = []\\n+    try:\\n+        for row_number, row in enumerate(rows, start=2):\\n+            raw_phone = (row.get(\\\"phone\\\") or \\\"\\\").strip()\\n+            phone = normalize_phone(raw_phone)\\n+            name = (row.get(\\\"name\\\") or \\\"\\\").strip()\\n+            price = (row.get(\\\"plan_price\\\") or \\\"\\\").strip()\\n+            start_value = (row.get(\\\"start_date\\\") or     try:\\n+                plan_price = Decimal(price)\\n+                start_date = parse_import_date(start_value)\\n+                if plan_price <= 0: raise ValueError(\\\"Plan price must be             created.append(notify_customer_due_for_delivery(db, assignment.customer, simulated_date))\n@@\n @app.post(\"/customers/{customer_id}/pause\", response_model=PauseResponse, Depends(current_user)):\n+    return db.query(Subscription).order_by(Subscription.id).all()\n+\n+\n+@app.get(\"/subscriptions/{subscription_id}\")\n+def get_subscription(subscription_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db), _: User = Depends(current_user)):\n+    subscription = db.get(Subscription, subscription_id)\n+    if not subscription:\n+        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"Subscription not found\")\n+    return {\"id\": subscription.id, \"plan_price\": subscription.plan_price, \"cycle_start\": subscription.cycle_start, \"cycle_end\": subscription.cycle_end, \"status\": subscription.status, \"assignments\": [{\"customer_id\": item.customer_id, \"customer_name\": item.customer.name, \"start_date\": item.start_date, \"end_date\": item.end_date} for item in subscription.assignments]}\n+\n+\n+@app.post(\"/subscriptions/{subscription_id}/transfer\")\n+def transfer_subscription(subscription_id: int, payload: TransferRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db), _: User = Depends(current_user)):\n+    subscription = db.get(Subscription, subscription_id)\n+    new_customer = db.get(Customer, payload.new_customer_id)\n+    if not subscription:\n+        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"Subscription not found\")\n+    if not new_customer:\n+        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"New customer not found\")\n+    if not subscription.cycle_start <= payload.transfer_date <= subscription.cycle_end:\n+        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"Transfer date must be inside the subscription cycle\")\n+    current = next((item for item in subscription.assignments if item.start_date <= payload.transfer_date <= item.end_date), None)\n+    if not current:\n+        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=\"No current owner exists on the transfer date\")\n+    if current.customer_id == new_customer.id:\n+        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=\"New customer already owns the subscription\")\n+    current.end_date = payload.transfer_date - timedelta(days=1)\n+    db.add(SubscriptionAssignment(subscription_id=subscription.id, customer_id=new_customer.id, start_date=payload.transfer_date, end_date=subscription.cycle_end))\n+    db.commit()\n+    return {\"message\": \"Subscription transferred\", \"subscription_id\": subscription.id, \"old_customer_id\": current.customer_id, \"new_customer_id\": new_customer.id, \"transfer_date\": payload.transfer_date}\n+\n+\n+def parse_import_date(value: str) -> date:\n+    value = value.strip()\n+    formats = [\"%Y-%m-%d\", \"%d/%m/%Y\"]\n+    if re.fullmatch(r\"\\d{2}-\\d{2}-\\d{4}\", value):\n+        day, month, year = map(int, value.split(\"-\"))\n+        if day <= 12 and month <= 12:\n+            raise ValueError(\"Ambiguous date\")\n+        return date(year, month, day)\n+    for fmt in formats:\n+        try:\n+            return __import__(\"datetime\").datetime.strptime(value, fmt).date()\n+        except ValueError:\n+            pass\n+    raise ValueError(\"Invalid date\")\n+\n+\n+@app.post(\"/customers/import\", response_model=ImportReport)\n+def import_customers(file: UploadFile = File(...), db: Session = Depends(get_db), _: User = Depends(current_user)):\n+    try:\n+        rows = list(csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(file.file.read().decode(\"utf-8-sig\"))))\n+    except UnicodeDecodeError:\n+        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"CSV must be UTF-8 text\")\n+    imported = 0; deduped = 0; rejected = 0; seen = set(); deduped_details = []; rejected_details = []\n+    try:\n+        for row_number, row in enumerate(rows, start=2):\n+            raw_phone = (row.get(\"phone\") or \"\").strip()\n+            phone = normalize_phone(raw_phone)\n+            name = (row.get(\"name\") or \"\").strip()\n+            price = (row.get(\"plan_price\") or \"\").strip()\n+            start_value = (row.get(\"start_date\") or row.get(\"subscription_start_date\") or \"\").strip()\n+            if not phone:\n+                rejected += 1; rejected_details.append(ImportDetail(row=row_number, reason=\"Missing phone\")); continue\n+            if not name:\n+                rejected += 1; rejected_details.append(ImportDetail(row=row_number, phone=phone, reason=\"Missing name\")); continue\n+            try:\n+                plan_price = Decimal(price)\n+                start_date = parse_import_date(start_value)\n+                if plan_price <= 0: raise ValueError(\"Plan price must be positive\")\n+            except (ValueError, ArithmeticError) as error:\n+                rejected += 1; rejected_details.append(ImportDetail(row=row_number, phone=phone, reason=str(error))); continue\n+            if phone in seen or db.query(Customer).filter(Customer.phone == phone).first():\n+                deduped += 1; deduped_details.append(ImportDetail(row=row_number, phone=phone, reason=\"Duplicate phone\")); continue\n+            seen.add(phone)\n+            customer = Customer(name=name, phone=phone, address=(row.get(\"address\") or \"\").strip(), plan_price=plan_price, subscription_start_date=start_date)\n+            db.add(customer); db.flush(); create_subscription_for_customer(db, customer); imported += 1\n+        db.commit()\n+    except Exception:\n+        db.rollback()\n+        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"Import transaction failed\")\n+    return ImportReport(imported=imported, deduped=deduped, rejected=rejected, details={\"deduped\": deduped_details, \"rejected\": rejected_details})\n*** End Patch","explanation":"Implement idempotent delivery notifications, transfer history, subscription inspection, and transactional CSV import {"type":"tool.execution_start","data":{"toolCallId":"call_6yjldEk0kkYatGH4pVdP85Di","toolName":"create_file","arguments":{"filePath":"/workspaces/Tiffinsub/backend/tests/test_twists.py","content":"from datetime import date\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom types import SimpleNamespace\nfrom app.billing import calculate_subscription_bill\n\n\ndef assignment(customer_id, name, start, end, pauses=()):\n    customer = SimpleNamespace(id=customer_id, name=name, pauses=list(pauses))\n    return SimpleNamespace(customer_id=customer_id, customer=customer, start_date=start, end_date=end)\n\n\ndef test_transfer_billing_splits_same_cycle_and_pause():\n    pause = SimpleNamespace(start_date=date(2026, 9, 18), end_date=date(2026, 9, 18))\n    subscription = SimpleNamespace(\n        id=1,\n        plan_price=Decimal(\"2200.00\"),\n        cycle_start=date(2026, 9, 1),\n        cycle_end=date(2026, 9, 30),\n        assignments=[\n            assignment(1, \"A\", date(2026, 9, 1), date(2026, 9, 15)),\n            assignment(2, \"B\", date(2026, 9, 16), date(2026, 9, 30), [pause]),\n        ],\n    )\n    result = calculate_subscription_bill(subscription, 2026, 9)\n    assert result[\"total_weekdays\"] == 22\n    assert result[\"customers\"][0][\"delivered_weekdays\"] == 11\n    assert result[\"customers\"][1][\"delivered_weekdays\"] == 10\n    assert result[\"customers\"][0][\"amount\"] == Decimal(\"1100.00\")\n    assert result[\"customers\"][1][\"amount\"] == Decimal(\"1000.00\")\n\n\ndef test_weekend_clock_dates_have_no_due_delivery():\n    assert date(2026, 9, 19).weekday() >= 5\n"}},"id":"0c5bb23c-9050-4afd-93eb-463f38779e5a","timestamp":"2026-09-17T10:40:07.064Z","parentId":"d73a3c1c-5569-4c0c-9178-547ac035a594"}

assert r.status_code==201,r.text\\nh={'Authorization':'Bearer '+r.json()['access_token']}\\ndef customer(name,phone):\\n r=client.post('/customers',headers=h,json={'name':name,'phone':phone,'address':'','plan_price':2200,'subscription_start_date':'2026-09-01'}); assert r.status_code==201,r.text; return r.json()\\na=customer('A','9876543210'); b=customer('B','9999999999')\\nsubs=client.get('/subscriptions',headers=h); assert subs.status_code==200 and len(subs.json())==2\\nsid=subs.json()[0]['id']\\nr=client.post(f'/subscriptions/{sid}/transfer',headers=h,json={'new_customer_id':b['id'],'transfer_date':'2026-09-16'}); assert r.status_code==200,r.text\\nr=client.post('/clock',json={'date':'2026-09-17'}); assert r.status_code==200 and r.json()['notified']==1,r.text\\nout=client.get('/outbox'); assert len(out.json())==1 and out.json()[0]['customer_id']==b['id'],out.text\\ncsv=b'name,phone,plan_price,start_date\\\\nRahul,987-654-3210,2200,2026-09-01\\\\nPriya,,2200,2026-09-02\\\\nAmit,9999999999,,09-05-2026\\\\nNeha,8888888888,2500,2026-09-10\\\\n'\\nr=client.post('/customers/import',headers=h,files={'file':('customers.csv',BytesIO(csv),'text/csv')}); assert r.status_code==200,r.text\\nreport=r.json(); assert report['imported']==1 and report['deduped']==1 and report['rejected']==2,report\\nprint('twist integration flow passed:', report)\\nPY\\nPYTHONPATH=backend pytest -q backend/tests\",\"explanation\":\"Run a fresh end-to-end API scenario covering registration, transfer, clock/outbox idempotent workflow, and messy CSV import persistence/reporting.\",\"goal\":\"Verify all twist workflows\",\"mode\":\"sync\",\"timeout\":120000}","type":"function"}],"reasoningText":""},"id":"f10ce396-fa92-4649-ba6d-46542e741642","timestamp":"2026-09-17T10:40:37.533Z","parentId":"133dcf99-1b1a-4a9e-a9ee-620e1824c90a"}

assert r.status_code==201,r.text\nh={'Authorization':'Bearer '+r.json()['access_token']}\ndef customer(name,phone):\n r=client.post('/customers',headers=h,json={'name':name,'phone':phone,'address':'','plan_price':2200,'subscription_start_date':'2026-09-01'}); assert r.status_code==201,r.text; return r.json()\na=customer('A','9876543210'); b=customer('B','9999999999')\nsubs=client.get('/subscriptions',headers=h); assert subs.status_code==200 and len(subs.json())==2\nsid=subs.json()[0]['id']\nr=client.post(f'/subscriptions/{sid}/transfer',headers=h,json={'new_customer_id':b['id'],'transfer_date':'2026-09-16'}); assert r.status_code==200,r.text\nr=client.post('/clock',json={'date':'2026-09-17'}); assert r.status_code==200 and r.json()['notified']==1,r.text\nout=client.get('/outbox'); assert len(out.json())==1 and out.json()[0]['customer_id']==b['id'],out.text\ncsv=b'name,phone,plan_price,start_date\\nRahul,987-654-3210,2200,2026-09-01\\nPriya,,2200,2026-09-02\\nAmit,9999999999,,09-05-2026\\nNeha,8888888888,2500,2026-09-10\\n'\nr=client.post('/customers/import',headers=h,files={'file':('customers.csv',BytesIO(csv),'text/csv')}); assert r.status_code==200,r.text\nreport=r.json(); assert report['imported']==1 and report['deduped']==1 and report['rejected']==2,report\nprint('twist integration flow passed:', report)\nPY\nPYTHONPATH=backend pytest -q backend/tests","explanation":"Run a fresh end-to-end API scenario covering registration, transfer, clock/outbox idempotent workflow, and messy CSV import persistence/reporting.","goal":"Verify all twist workflows","mode":"sync","timeout":120000}},"id":"72e6aa13-6161-41fb-82d4-8ceb54140ad7","timestamp":"2026-09-17T10:40:37.789Z","parentId":"07a5888f-cc1a-478e-b1dd-723571c14fef"}

{"type":"assistant.message","data":{"messageId":"82c3b304-ecd2-409a-9818-ed404dac4b98","content":"The backend twist workflow is now passing end to end: transfer preserves assignment history, `/clock` produces idempotent `/outbox` notifications, and CSV import persists valid rows while reporting duplicates/rejections. I’m documenting the new contracts and doing the final build/import checks; the core evaluator path is now backend-complete.","toolRequests":[{"toolCallId":"call_PZYnqoKsPIbcGJpZMW3vxbaQ","name":"apply_patch","arguments":"{\"input\":\"*** Begin Patch\\n*** Update File: /workspaces/Tiffinsub/README.md\\n@@\\n | GET | `/customers/{id}/bill` | Calculate monthly bill | `month=YYYY-MM` |\\n+| POST | `/clock` | Simulate a date and notify due customers | `{date?: \\\"YYYY-MM-DD\\\"}` |\\n+| GET | `/outbox` | Inspect delivery notifications | none |\\n+| GET | `/subscriptions` | List subscription cycles | none |\\n+| GET | `/subscriptions/{id}` | Inspect assignments/history | none |\\n+| POST | `/subscriptions/{id}/transfer` | Transfer ownership mid-cycle | `{new_customer_id,transfer_date}` |\\n+| POST | `/customers/import` | Import and normalize CSV customers | multipart `file` |\\n@@\\n List responses include `data`, `page`, `limit`, `total`, and `total_pages`. Invalid ranges return 400, duplicates and overlapping pauses return 409, missing customers return 404, and missing/invalid tokens return 401.\\n+\\n+`POST /clock` is safe to repeat for the same date: it uses a unique customer/date outbox key. It only notifies weekday assignments that are inside their ownership period and outside customer pauses. Transfers preserve the original subscription cycle and split billing by `SubscriptionAssignment` ownership periods. CSV import accepts `YYYY-MM-DD` and `DD/MM/YYYY`; ambiguous hyphen dates are rejected rather than guessed. The report returns `imported`, `deduped`, `rejected`, and row details.\\n*** Update File: /workspaces/Tiffinsub/REASONING.md\\n@@\\n Customers have a unique phone number. Pauses belong to customers and overlapping ranges are rejected so billing never double-counts an ambiguous range. Money is stored as a SQL numeric value and calculated with Decimal rounding to cents.\\n+\\n+The lifecycle model also includes a Subscription cycle and SubscriptionAssignment ownership periods. A transfer closes the old assignment the day before the transfer and starts the new assignment on the transfer date, preserving the plan and cycle. OutboxNotification records provide a local Notification Service adapter and a unique customer/date key for idempotent clock runs. ClockState persists simulated dates.\\n@@\\n ## Trade-offs\\n@@\\n The MVP uses a fixed local JWT secret and a simple date-based resume operation. Production work would move secrets to environment variables, add migrations, richer authorization, API integration tests, and customer-facing notifications. The UI intentionally favors the owner workflow over a larger design system.\\n+\\n+## Twist verification\\n+\\n+The twist integration scenario was run against a fresh SQLite database: it registered an owner, created two customers, transferred one subscription, advanced `/clock` to a weekday, verified one `/outbox` notification, imported a messy CSV, and verified `imported=1`, `deduped=1`, and `rejected=2`. The automated suite reports four passing billing/twist tests.\\n*** End Patch\",\"explanation\":\"Document the new clock/outbox, transfer, assignment billing, and CSV import contracts and verified twist scenario.\"}","type":"function"}],"reasoningText":""},"id":"bf9c7ab3-2ef0-4b35-a6db-063086913afe","timestamp":"2026-09-17T10:45:56.991Z","parentId":"54f2939d-f784-4fb0-8cb2-bc3247ff5e62"}

Invalid ranges return 400, duplicates and overlapping pauses return 409, missing customers return 404, and missing/invalid tokens return 401.\n+\n+`POST /clock` is safe to repeat for the same date: it uses a unique customer/date outbox key. It only notifies weekday assignments that are inside their ownership period and outside customer pauses. Transfers preserve the original subscription cycle and split billing by `SubscriptionAssignment` ownership periods. CSV import accepts `YYYY-MM-DD` and `DD/MM/YYYY`; ambiguous hyphen dates are rejected rather than guessed. The report returns `imported`, `deduped`, `rejected`, and row details.\n*** Update File: /workspaces/Tiffinsub/REASONING.md\n@@\n Customers have a unique phone number. Pauses belong to customers and overlapping ranges are rejected so billing never double-counts an ambiguous range. Money is stored as a SQL numeric value and calculated with Decimal rounding to cents.\n+\n+The lifecycle model also includes a Subscription cycle and SubscriptionAssignment ownership periods. A transfer closes the old assignment the day before the transfer and starts the new assignment on the transfer date, preserving the plan and cycle. OutboxNotification records provide a local Notification Service adapter and a unique customer/date key for idempotent clock runs. ClockState persists simulated dates.\n@@\n ## Trade-offs\n@@\n The MVP uses a fixed local JWT secret and a simple date-based resume operation. Production work would move secrets to environment variables, add migrations, richer authorization, API integration tests, and customer-facing notifications. The UI intentionally favors the owner workflow over a larger design system.\n+\n+## Twist verification\n+\n+The twist integration scenario was run against a fresh SQLite database: it registered an owner, created two customers, transferred one subscription, advanced `/clock` to a weekday, verified one `/outbox` notification, imported a messy CSV, and verified `imported=1`, `deduped=1`, and `rejected=2`. The automated suite reports four passing billing/twist tests.\n*** End Patch","explanation":"Document the new clock/outbox, transfer, assignment billing, and CSV import contracts and verified twist scenario."}},"id":"5ca94657-ea6c-422f-8b02-de8cfd81ff9e","timestamp":"2026-09-17T10:45:57.226Z","parentId":"e14c0388-1619-43b0-98ab-1ce170baf562"}

{"type":"assistant.message","data":{"messageId":"5ef21109-1d5d-4527-8d7e-32d533c950ae","content":"","toolRequests":[{"toolCallId":"call_Sqh6vjCMIcNrPMUn9N5G5QXA","name":"apply_patch","arguments":"{\"input\":\"*** Begin Patch\\n*** Update File: /workspaces/Tiffinsub/backend/app/main.py\\n@@\\n @app.get(\\\"/subscriptions/{subscription_id}\\\")\\n def get_subscription(subscription_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db), _: User = Depends(current_user)):\\n@@\\n     return {\\\"id\\\": subscription.id, \\\"plan_price\\\": subscription.plan_price, \\\"cycle_start\\\": subscription.cycle_start, \\\"cycle_end\\\": subscription.cycle_end, \\\"status\\\": subscription.status, \\\"assignments\\\": [{\\\"customer_id\\\": item.customer_id, \\\"customer_name\\\": item.customer.name, \\\"start_date\\\": item.start_date, \\\"end_date\\\": item.end_date} for item in subscription.assignments]}\\n+\\n+\\n+@app.get(\\\"/subscriptions/{subscription_id}/bill\\\")\\n+def get_subscription_bill(subscription_id: int, month: str = Query(..., pattern=r\\\"^\\\\d{4}-\\\\d{2}$\\\"), db: Session = Depends(get_db), _: User = Depends(current_user)):\\n+    subscription = db.get(Subscription, subscription_id)\\n+    if not subscription:\\n+        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\\\"Subscription not found\\\")\\n+    try:\\n+        year, month_number = map(int, month.split(\\\"-\\\")); date(year, month_number, 1)\\n+    except ValueError:\\n+        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\\\"Month must be YYYY-MM\\\")\\n+    return calculate_subscription_bill(subscription, year, month_number)\\n*** End Patch\",\"explanation\":\"Expose the transfer-aware subscription billing result directly so evaluators can inspect the split by ownership period.\"}","type":"function"}],"reasoningText":""},"id":"d74204c1-a4a4-495d-ae93-875c6813559e","timestamp":"2026-09-17T10:46:59.180Z","parentId":"b0a17f22-c0ec-4bf7-aa1a-79bf33ca7762"}

{"type":"tool.execution_start","data":{"toolCallId":"call_Sqh6vjCMIcNrPMUn9N5G5QXA","toolName":"apply_patch","arguments":{"input":"*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /workspaces/Tiffinsub/backend/app/main.py\n@@\n @app.get(\"/subscriptions/{subscription_id}\")\n def get_subscription(subscription_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db), _: User = Depends(current_user)):\n@@\n     return {\"id\": subscription.id, \"plan_price\": subscription.plan_price, \"cycle_start\": subscription.cycle_start, \"cycle_end\": subscription.cycle_end, \"status\": subscription.status, \"assignments\": [{\"customer_id\": item.customer_id, \"customer_name\": item.customer.name, \"start_date\": item.start_date, \"end_date\": item.end_date} for item in subscription.assignments]}\n+\n+\n+@app.get(\"/subscriptions/{subscription_id}/bill\")\n+def get_subscription_bill(subscription_id: int, month: str = Query(..., pattern=r\"^\\d{4}-\\d{2}$\"), db: Session = Depends(get_db), _: User = Depends(current_user)):\n+    subscription = db.get(Subscription, subscription_id)\n+    if not subscription:\n+        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"Subscription not found\")\n+    try:\n+        year, month_number = map(int, month.split(\"-\")); date(year, month_number, 1)\n+    except ValueError:\n+        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"Month must be YYYY-MM\")\n+    return calculate_subscription_bill(subscription, year, month_number)\n*** End Patch","explanation":"Expose the transfer-aware subscription billing result directly so evaluators can inspect the split by ownership period."}},"id":"6205d8c2-a079-48d3-a1db-53cf850c64b7","timestamp":"2026-09-17T10:46:59.397Z","parentId":"fd1b7e8f-434d-4764-b5e9-48647d07e8cc"}

{"type":"assistant.message","data":{"messageId":"751a5612-a8fd-48f4-a1e1-c418b3e98e83","content":"Implemented the three required twists in the backend.\n\n**T1: Clock and notifications**\n- `POST /clock`\n- `GET /outbox`\n- Weekends excluded\n- Paused customers excluded\n- Future subscriptions excluded\n- Repeated clock calls are idempotent\n- Persistent outbox notifications\n\n**T6: Subscription transfer**\n- Added `subscriptions`\n- Added `subscription_assignments`\n- Added:\n\n```text\nPOST /subscriptions/{id}/transfer\nGET  /subscriptions/{id}\nGET  /subscriptions/{id}/bill?month=2026-09\n```\n\n- Original cycle and plan are preserved\n- Ownership history is preserved\n- Billing splits by customer assignment period\n- Customer pauses still affect the correct owner\n\n**T4: CSV import**\n- Added:\n\n```text\nPOST /customers/import\n```\n\n- Phone normalization\n- Duplicate detection\n- Date normalization\n- Invalid-row rejection\n- Persistent valid customer/subscription creation\n- Report containing:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"imported\": 1,\n  \"deduped\": 1,\n  \"rejected\": 2\n}\n```\n\n**Verification**\n\n- Twist integration flow passed\n- Billing and twist tests: `4 passed`\n- Frontend build passes\n- New routes confirmed:\n\n```text\n/clock\n/outbox\n/subscriptions\n/subscriptions/{id}\n/subscriptions/{id}/bill\n/subscriptions/{id}/transfer\n/customers/import\n```\n\nOpen API documentation:\n\nhttp://localhost:5174/api/docs\n\nOne remaining limitation: the CSV import backend is complete, but the existing dashboard does not yet expose a visible “Import CSV” button. The import API can be tested directly from Swagger or with `curl`.","toolRequests":[],"reasoningText":""},"id":"e3e38c71-9eea-4957-bf02-3deb13f0a604","timestamp":"2026-09-17T10:47:34.793Z","parentId":"98b8afe0-c29c-456a-ae23-fd6836516730"}

{"type":"assistant.message","data":{"messageId":"f58d8405-aa99-4e12-a519-22f77df0fd81","content":"No code was modified.\n\n**Sources**\n\nThe main behavior is in [backend/app/main.py](backend/app/main.py), billing is in [backend/app/billing.py](backend/app/billing.py), persistence is in [backend/app/models.py](backend/app/models.py), notifications are in [backend/app/notification_service.py](backend/app/notification_service.py), and the frontend controls are in [frontend/src/App.jsx](frontend/src/App.jsx).\n\nThe existing backend tests pass: `4 passed`.\n\n## Current Pause Model\n\n### 1. How a customer is marked paused\n\nA customer is paused by inserting a `SubscriptionPause` row:\n\n```text\ncustomer_id\nstart_date\nend_date\ncreated_at\n```\n\nThere is no `paused` boolean column on `Customer`.\n\nThe customer is considered paused when:\n\n```python\npause.start_date <= today <= pause.end_date\n```\n\nThis is implemented by `customer_status()` in [backend/app/main.py](backend/app/main.py).\n\n### 2. What happens in the database when pausing\n\n`POST /customers/{id}/pause`:\n\n1. Loads the `Customer`.\n2. Rejects an end date before the start date.\n3. Searches for an overlapping `SubscriptionPause`.\n4. Creates a new row in `subscription_pauses`.\n5. Commits the transaction.\n6. Returns the inserted pause.\n\nIt does not modify:\n\n- The customer row\n- The subscription row\n- Subscription assignments\n- Existing notifications\n- Billing records, because billing is calculated dynamically\n\nThe relationship is configured with delete cascading, so deleting a customer deletes its pauses.\n\n### 3. What happens when resuming\n\n`POST /customers/{id}/resume` finds every pause active on the server's real current date.\n\nFor each active pause, it changes:\n\n```text\nend_date = today - 1 day\n```\n\nIt then commits and returns:\n\n```json\n{\"message\": \"Customer resumed\"}\n```\n\nResume does not delete the pause history.\n\n### 4. Are dates inclusive or exclusive?\n\nInclusive.\n\nA pause from September 10 through September 12 includes all three calendar dates:\n\n```text\nSeptember 10\nSeptember 11\nSeptember 12\n```\n\nThe same inclusive comparison is used for:\n\n- Customer status\n- `/clock` delivery eligibility\n- Billing range intersection\n\n### 5. How multiple pause periods work\n\nMultiple pause rows are supported.\n\nBilling creates a set of paused weekdays, so separate non-overlapping periods are combined correctly.\n\nExample:\n\n```text\nPause 1: Sep 1 to Sep 3\nPause 2: Sep 10 to Sep 12\n```\n\nBoth ranges affect billing.\n\n### 6. What happens if periods overlap?\n\nThe pause API rejects the new period with HTTP `409`:\n\n```json\n{\"detail\": \"Pause overlaps an existing pause\"}\n```\n\nThe overlap test is inclusive. Even touching on the same date counts as overlap.\n\nAdjacent periods are allowed if they do not share a date:\n\n```text\nSep 1 to Sep 3\nSep 4 to Sep 5\n```\n\nThere is no database-level constraint preventing overlaps if rows are inserted another way.\n\n### 7. What happens with a one-day pause?\n\nA one-day pause is valid because `start_date == end_date` is allowed.\n\nIf it is a weekday, it removes one delivery day and one prorated billing day.\n\nIf it is a weekend, it removes no delivery day and no billing day, because weekends are never counted.\n\nThe customer can still show as `PAUSED` on that weekend date.\n\n## Billing Behavior\n\nBilling is implemented in [backend/app/billing.py](backend/app/billing.py).\n\n### 8. Pause starts before the billing month and ends inside it\n\nOnly the part inside the selected month is considered.\n\nExample:\n\n```text\nPause: Aug 25 to Sep 5\nBilling month: September\n```\n\nOnly September 1 through September 5 can affect September billing. Weekends inside that range are ignored.\n\n### 9. Pause starts inside the month and ends after it\n\nAgain, only the part inside the month is considered.\n\nExample:\n\n```text\nPause: Sep 20 to Oct 5\nBilling month: September\n```\n\nOnly September 20 through September 30 can affect September billing.\n\n### 10. What happens on weekends?\n\nWeekends are never delivery days.\n\n`weekdays_in_range()` includes only Monday through Friday. Therefore:\n\n- `/clock` does not notify anyone on weekends.\n- Weekend pauses do not reduce billing.\n- Weekend dates still belong to the inclusive pause range.\n- A customer can show `PAUSED` on a weekend.\n\n### 11. How TODAY status is calculated\n\n`customer_status()` uses Python's real:\n\n```python\ndate.today()\n```\n\nIt does not use the persisted `ClockState`.\n\nTherefore, the dashboard's `ACTIVE` or `PAUSED` status is based on the actual server date, not the date most recently supplied to `/clock`.\n\n### 12. How pause status affects today's delivery\n\n`POST /clock` checks:\n\n```python\nassignment.start_date <= simulated_date <= assignment.end_date\n```\n\nand also checks that the simulated date is not inside any customer pause.\n\nIf the customer is paused on that simulated weekday:\n\n- No notification is created.\n- The customer is not counted in `notified`.\n\n### 13. How pause status affects monthly billing\n\nBilling calculates:\n\n```text\namount = plan_price / total_month_weekdays * delivered_weekdays\n```\n\nPaused weekdays are removed from `delivered_weekdays`.\n\nFor a customer without an assignment, the fallback path also removes paused days from the customer's service days.\n\nFor an assigned subscription, billing uses the assignment's service period and the assigned customer's pauses.\n\n### 14. Interaction with subscription start/end dates\n\nA customer's subscription starts at `subscription_start_date`.\n\nCreating a customer creates:\n\n- A `Subscription`\n- A `SubscriptionAssignment`\n\nThe initial subscription cycle ends on the last day of the customer's start month.\n\nBilling only counts weekdays within:\n\n```text\nassignment start\nsubscription cycle start\nselected month start\n```\n\nthrough:\n\n```text\nassignment end\nsubscription cycle end\nselected month end\n```\n\nHowever, customer status does not check subscription dates. A future-start customer can still appear `ACTIVE`, and a pause outside the subscription period can still make the customer appear `PAUSED` if it covers today.\n\nThere is no customer subscription end-date field.\n\n### 15. Interaction with subscription transfer / T6\n\nThere is no code explicitly named `T6`, but the transfer behavior is implemented by:\n\n```text\nPOST /subscriptions/{subscription_id}/transfer\n```\n\nThe transfer:\n\n1. Ends the old assignment the day before the transfer.\n2. Creates a new assignment beginning on the transfer date.\n3. Keeps the same subscription cycle.\n\nBilling applies pauses according to the customer owning the assignment for that date.\n\nExample:\n\n- Customer A owns September 1–15.\n- Customer B owns September 16–30.\n- A pause belonging to B on September 18 affects B's delivery and billing.\n- A pause belonging to A after September 15 does not affect B.\n\nThe transfer route does not automatically create, change, or remove pause records.\n\n### 16. Interaction with daily notification / T1\n\nThere is no literal `T1` identifier in the code.\n\nThe equivalent behavior is:\n\n- `/clock` checks weekday status.\n- It checks assignment dates.\n- It excludes paused customers.\n- It creates an `OutboxNotification`.\n\nNotifications are stored in [backend/app/models.py](backend/app/models.py). Each customer/date pair is unique, so repeating `/clock` for the same date returns the existing notification rather than creating another one.\n\n## APIs\n\n### 17. Pause and resume endpoints\n\n```text\nPOST /customers/{id}/pause\nPOST /customers/{id}/resume\nGET  /customers/{id}/pauses\nPOST /clock\nGET  /customers/{id}/bill?month=YYYY-MM\n```\n\nCustomer endpoints require a bearer token.\n\n### 18. Example pause request and response\n\nRequest:\n\n```http\nPOST /customers/7/pause\nAuthorization: Bearer <token>\nContent-Type: application/json\n```\n\n```json\n{\n  \"start_date\": \"2026-09-14\",\n  \"end_date\": \"2026-09-16\"\n}\n```\n\nResponse, HTTP `201`:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"id\": 12,\n  \"start_date\": \"2026-09-14\",\n  \"end_date\": \"2026-09-16\"\n}\n```\n\nResume request:\n\n```http\nPOST /customers/7/resume\nAuthorization: Bearer <token>\n```\n\nResponse:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"message\": \"Customer resumed\"\n}\n```\n\nPause history request:\n\n```http\nGET /customers/7/pauses\nAuthorization: Bearer <token>\n```\n\nResponse:\n\n```json\n[\n  {\n    \"id\": 12,\n    \"start_date\": \"2026-09-14\",\n    \"end_date\": \"2026-09-16\"\n  }\n]\n```\n\n## September Example\n\nSubscription:\n\n```text\nPlan price: ₹2200\nSeptember weekdays: 22\nPaused weekdays: 3\n```\n\nFor example, pause:\n\n```text\nSeptember 14, 15, and 16\n```\n\nAll three are weekdays.\n\nCalculation:\n\n```text\nDelivered weekdays = 22 - 3 = 19\nDaily rate = ₹2200 / 22 = ₹100\nBill = 19 × ₹100 = ₹1900\n```\n\nSo the customer is billed:\n\n```text\n₹1900.00\n```\n\nThe billed days are the other 19 weekdays. Weekends were never part of the 22-day denominator and are not separately charged.\n\n## Internal Execution\n\n### 20. `POST /customers/{id}/pause`\n\nIn [backend/app/main.py](backend/app/main.py):\n\n1. FastAPI parses `start_date` and `end_date` using `PauseCreate`.\n2. The customer is loaded by ID.\n3. Missing customer returns `404`.\n4. Invalid date order returns `400`.\n5. Existing ranges are checked using inclusive overlap logic.\n6. An overlap returns `409`.\n7. A `SubscriptionPause` object is created.\n8. SQLAlchemy inserts it into `subscription_pauses`.\n9. The transaction is committed.\n10. The saved row is returned.\n\n### 21. `POST /customers/{id}/resume`\n\n1. The customer is loaded.\n2. Missing customer returns `404`.\n3. The code uses real `date.today()`.\n4. Every pause containing today is selected.\n5. Each selected pause's `end_date` is changed to yesterday.\n6. The changes are committed.\n7. A simple message is returned.\n\n### 22. `POST /clock` on a paused customer's day\n\nExample:\n\n```http\nPOST /clock\nContent-Type: application/json\n```\n\n```json\n{\n  \"date\": \"2026-09-15\"\n}\n```\n\nThe endpoint:\n\n1. Stores `2026-09-15` in `clock_state`.\n2. Checks whether it is a weekday.\n3. Loops through subscription assignments.\n4. Checks assignment start/end dates.\n5. Checks whether the date is inside a pause.\n6. Finds the customer paused.\n7. Skips notification creation.\n8. Commits the clock state.\n9. Returns:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"date\": \"2026-09-15\",\n  \"notified\": 0\n}\n```\n\nExisting notifications are not deleted.\n\n## Bugs and Ambiguities\n\n1. **Resume can create an invalid range.**  \n   If a pause starts today, resume changes its end date to yesterday, leaving:\n\n   ```text\n   start_date > end_date\n   ```\n\n2. **Status ignores subscription dates.**  \n   A pause can mark a customer `PAUSED` even when the customer has not started or the subscription has ended.\n\n3. **Status ignores simulated clock time.**  \n   `/clock` uses its supplied date, but customer status uses real `date.today()`.\n\n4. **No pause bounds validation.**  \n   The API allows pauses before subscription start, after the subscription cycle, or far in the future.\n\n5. **No pause editing or deletion endpoint exists.**\n\n6. **Overlapping pauses are only protected at the API level.**  \n   Direct database writes could create overlaps.\n\n7. **The subscription billing summary can be misleading with assignment gaps.**  \n   `paused_weekdays` is calculated as:\n\n   ```text\n   total_weekdays - delivered_total\n   ```\n\n   rather than strictly counting paused service weekdays.\n\n8. **The customer bill uses only the first subscription.**  \n   `calculate_monthly_bill()` selects the first subscription associated with a customer.\n\n9. **No actual notification delivery occurs.**  \n   Notifications are only placed into the local outbox table.\n\n10. **There is no integration test for pause/resume API behavior.**  \n    Existing tests cover billing and a transfer billing scenario, but not the HTTP routes, resume mutation, simulated clock/status mismatch, or invalid resume ranges.\n\n11. **The frontend does not refresh the pause history after resume.**  \n    It refreshes the customer list, but the open modal's local pause list is not reloaded.\n\n12. **The frontend uses real browser date defaults.**  \n    The dashboard displays a hard-coded “September 2026” label, while backend status uses the server date.\n\n","toolRequests":[],"reasoningText":""},"id":"f2615763-36dc-41d2-9124-bcba06f75fec","timestamp":"2026-09-17T11:12:46.311Z","parentId":"c9532d5a-b057-4c84-8bf2-1702a5a46963"}
{"type":"assistant.turn_end","data":{"turnId":"8"},"id":"357b2687-42b1-4455-9780-550984244287","timestamp":"2026-09-17T11:12:46.311Z","parentId":"f2615763-36dc-41d2-9124-bcba06f75fec"}
{"type":"user.message","data":{"content":"We also need a CUSTOMER/USER SIDE in addition to the existing OWNER/ADMIN side.\n\nIMPORTANT:\n\n- Do NOT rebuild the application from scratch.\n- Do NOT remove or break existing owner/admin functionality.\n- First inspect the existing frontend, backend APIs, authentication, database models, and current UI.\n- Then extend the existing application with a proper customer-facing experience.\nCUSTOMER SIDE REQUIREMENTS:\n\n1. CUSTOMER AUTHENTICATION\n\n- Customer can register and login.\n- Use the existing authentication system if possible.\n- Do not create a separate unnecessary auth architecture.\n\n1. CUSTOMER DASHBOARD\nAfter login, customer should see:\n\n- Name\n- Phone\n- Current subscription/plan\n- Monthly plan price\n- Subscription cycle start/end\n- Current status: ACTIVE or PAUSED\n- Today's delivery status\n- Current month's delivered days\n- Current month's paused days\n- Estimated/current bill\n\n1. PAUSE SUBSCRIPTION\nCustomer must be able to pause their own tiffin service.\nProvide:\n\n- Pause From date\n- Pause To date\n- Reason (optional)\n- Pause button\nShow confirmation before submitting.\n\nAfter successful pause:\n\n- Update backend/database through the proper REST API.\n- Show the pause period in the UI.\n- Status should reflect PAUSED when applicable.\n- No delivery should be considered due on paused days.\n- Paused weekdays must NOT be included in the customer's bill.\n\n1. RESUME\nCustomer should be able to resume according to the application's business rules.\nIMPORTANT:\n\n- Do not simply change a status flag if the backend uses date-range pause records.\n- Preserve pause history.\n- Make sure resume does not corrupt billing.\n\n1. PAUSE HISTORY\nCreate a section showing:\n\n- Pause start date\n- Pause end date\n- Status\n- Optional reason\n\n1. DELIVERY CALENDAR / STATUS\nCreate a simple monthly calendar or delivery history showing each relevant day as:\n\n- Delivered\n- Paused\n- Weekend / No delivery\n- Future\nThis does not need to be overly complex. Prioritize correctness and usability.\n\n1. BILLING\nCustomer should be able to view:\n\n- Monthly plan price\n- Total weekdays in cycle/month\n- Paused weekdays\n- Delivered weekdays\n- Calculated bill\n- Billing period\nUse the SAME backend billing logic as the owner side.\nDo NOT calculate billing independently in frontend.\n\n1. SUBSCRIPTION DETAILS\nCustomer can view:\n\n- Plan price\n- Cycle dates\n- Current assigned customer/subscriber\n- Subscription status\nIf transfer history exists because of T6, display the relevant assignment history appropriately.\n\n1. CUSTOMER PROFILE\nAllow customer to view/update basic profile information such as:\n\n- Name\n- Phone\n- Address\nRespect backend validation.\n\n1. NOTIFICATIONS\nShow today's delivery notification/status if available through the existing Notification Service/outbox flow.\n2. SECURITY\nA customer must NEVER be able to:\n\n- View another customer's data\n- Pause another customer's subscription\n- Modify another customer's subscription\n- Access owner/admin-only endpoints\nEnforce authorization in the BACKEND, not just by hiding frontend buttons.\n\n1. OWNER ↔ CUSTOMER CONSISTENCY\nThis is extremely important.\nIf customer pauses from the customer UI:\nCustomer UI\n→ REST API\n→ Database\n→ Owner dashboard must immediately reflect the correct status.\n\nSimilarly:\nOwner pauses/resumes/transfers a subscription\n→ Customer dashboard must reflect the correct state.\n\nThere must be ONE source of truth: the backend/database.\n\n1. RESPONSIVE UI\nMake the customer side clean and usable on desktop and mobile.\nSuggested navigation:\n\n- Dashboard\n- My Subscription\n- Pause/Resume\n- Billing\n- Delivery History\n- Profile\n\n1. DEMO FLOW\nMake sure this complete flow works:\nRegister/Login as customer\n→ View active subscription\n→ Pause for selected dates\n→ See PAUSED status\n→ Verify those days are excluded from billing\n→ Resume\n→ See ACTIVE status again\n→ View updated delivery/billing information.\n\n1. IMPORTANT BUSINESS RULE\nDo not invent a different pause/billing model.\nUse the existing problem statement:\nA tiffin customer gets lunch every weekday.\nIf they pause for some days, they should not be charged for those paused delivery days.\nAt month-end, billing is based on the days actually served.\n\nBefore coding, inspect the existing implementation and explain briefly:\n\n- which existing APIs can be reused,\n- which new APIs are required,\n- which database changes are required,\n- which frontend pages/components need to be added.\nThen IMPLEMENT it, run the application, test the complete customer flow, fix errors, and ensure the existing owner/admin side still works.\n\nFinally update README.md with the customer-side flow and APIs.","attachments":[]},"id":"9809860b-62a7-4398-b4b2-0f3427b9c22a","timestamp":"2026-09-17T11:13:12.467Z","parentId":"357b2687-42b1-4455-9780-550984244287"}

'/auth/customer/register' : mode === 'register' ? '/auth/register' : '/auth/login'\\n      const body = customer ? form : mode === 'register' ? form : { email: form.email, password: form.password }\\n      const result = await api(path, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) })\\n      localStorage.setItem('tiffin_token', result.access_token)\\n      localStorage.setItem('tiffin_role', customer ? 'CUSTOMER' : 'OWNER')\\n      done()\\n    } catch (caught) { setError(caught.message) }\\n  }\\n  return <main className=\\\"auth\\\"><button className=\\\"text-button\\\" onClick={back}>← Back</button><form onSubmit={submit}>\\n    <b className=\\\"brand\\\"><i>TL</i> Tiffin Ledger</b><small>{customer ? 'CUSTOMER ACCESS' : 'OWNER ACCESS'}</small>\\n    <h1>{customer ? 'Join your lunch plan.' : mode === 'register' ? 'Start your ledger.' : 'Welcome back.'}</h1>\\n    {customer && <label>Phone<input required value={form.phone} onChange={event => setForm({ ...form, phone: event.target.value })} placeholder=\\\"The phone on your subscription\\\" /></label>}\\n    {(!customer && mode === 'register' || customer) && <label>Name<input required value={form.name} onChange={event => setForm({ ...form, name: event.target.value })} /></label>}\\n    <label>Email<input required type=\\\"email\\\" value={form.email} onChange={event => setForm({ ...form, email: event.target.value })} /></label>\\n    <label>Password<input required minLength=\\\"6\\\" type=\\\"password\\\" value={form.password} onChange={event => setForm({ ...form, password: event.target.value })} /></label>\\n    {error && <em>{error}</em>}<button className=\\\"primary\\\" type=\\\"submit\\\">{customer ? 'Create customer account' : mode === 'register' ? 'Create owner account' : 'Sign in'} →</button>\\n    {!customer && <button type=\\\"button\\\" className=\\\"text-button\\\" onClick={() => done(mode === 'register' ? 'login' : 'register')}>{mode === 'register' ? 'Already have an account? Sign in' : 'New owner? Every rupee accounted for.</h1><p>A shared tiffin ledger where owners manage service and customers can pause, resume, and understand every bill.</p><button className=\\\"primary\\\" onClick={() => choose('customer-register')}>Customer portal →</button></div><aside><small>THIS MONTH</small><strong>22</strong><span>weekday delivery days</span><hr /><small>Plans flex around real life. Your bill does too.</small></aside></section></main>\\n}\\n\\nfunction OwnerDashboard({ logout }) {\\n  const [customers, setCustomers] = useState({ data: [], total: 0, page: 1, total_pages: 0, limit: 6 })\\n  const [query, setQuery] = useState('')\\n  const [sort, setSort] = useState('name')\\n  const [detail, setDetail] = useState(null)\\n  const [add, setAdd] = useState(false)\\n  const load = (page = 1) => api(`/customers?page=${page}&limit=6&phone=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&sort_by=${sort}`).then(setCustomers).catch(() => {})\\n  useEffect(() => { load() }, [query, sort])\\n  return <main className=\\\"workspace owner-workspace\\\"><nav><b className=\\\"brand\\\"><i>TL</i> Tiffin Ledger</b><button className=\\\"text-button\\\" onClick={logout}>Log out</button></nav><div const pause = async event => { event.preventDefault(); try { await api(`/customers/${customer.id}/pause`, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(form) }); setPauses(await
